divisions.{{Cite web |last=Guillen |first=Adam |date=2024-10-17 |title=PFL\u2019s 2024 million-dollar Championship mega-event headed to Riyadh on Nov. 29 |language=en}} \n\nAt the preliminary card took place the four finals of [[2024 in Professional Fighters League#PFL MENA|2024 PFL MENA Tournament]].{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=PFL Championships, PFL MENA Finals Set for Saudi Arabia on Nov. 29 \n|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/PFL-Championships-PFL-MENA-Finals-Set-for-Saudi-Arabia-on-Nov-29-195269 |access-date=2024-10-21 |title=Full card for PFL MENA championships unveiled \n|url=https://english.alarabiya.net/sports/2024/11/12/full-card-for-pfl-mena-championships-unveiled |access-date=2024-11-28 |website=Al Arabiya English |language=en}} In turn, Alhyasat withdrew due to a \"urgent health problem\" and was replaced by Maraoune Bellagouit.{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=PFL MENA 4 Weigh-in Results: New Main |website=Sherdog |language=en}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Lilia Osmani came in at 108.3 lb, 2.3 pounds over the atomweight limit and she was fined a percentage that went to Hattan Alsaif. [[2023 in Professional Fighters League#2023 PFL Featherweight playoffs|2023 PFL featherweight]] winner [[Jesus Pinedo]] reportedly came in 6 pounds heavy for his featherweight against [[Jeremy Kennedy]].{{Cite web|url=https://bvmsports.com/2024/11/28/gabriel-braga-takes-on-jeremy-kennedy-after-pinedos-weight-miss/|title=Gabriel Braga Takes on Jeremy Kennedy After Pinedo\u2019s Weight Miss|publisher=bvmsports.com|author=BVM Sportsdesk|date=2024-11-28|access-date=2024-11-29}} As a result, Pinedo pulled out and was replaced by Gabriel Alves Braga on a day's notice.{{Cite web |date=2024-11-28 |title=2024 PFL Championship weigh-in results: Six $1 million title bouts set \n|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/11/2024-pfl-championship-brendan-loughnane-timur-khizriev-ditcheva-santos-weigh-in-results |access-date=2024-11-28 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card - Europe 1 (2024)|PFL Europe 1]]\n|followingevent=[[PFL 2 (2024)|PFL 2]]\n}}\n{{Main|2024 in Professional Fighters League}}\n\nThe '''''PFL 1''''' [[mixed martial arts]] event for the 2024 season of the [[Professional Fighters League]] was held on April 4, 2024, at the [[Port San Antonio#Boeing Center at Tech Port|Boeing Center at Tech Port]] in [[San Antonio, Texas]], United States. This marked the first regular-season event of the tournament and included fights in the [[Flyweight (MMA)|Women's Flyweight]] and [[Heavyweight (MMA)|Heavyweight]] divisions.{{Cite web |last=Cruz |first=Guilherme |date=2024-03-11 |title=PFL announces full line-ups for first 3 season events, 8 ex-Bellator champions debut |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/3/11/24097668/pfl-announces-full-line-up-three-season-events-debut-bellator-champions |access-date=2024-03-12 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}{{Cite web |date=2024-03-11 |title=PFL announces first three 2024 regular season lineups with influx of Bellator fighters, new women's division |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/2024-pfl-first-half-season-lineups-announced-bellator-influx-womens-flyweight-debut |access-date=2024-03-12 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=PFL Announces Full Cards for First Three Regular Season Events |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/PFL-Announces-Full-Cards-for-First-Three-Regular-Season-Events-192933 |access-date=2024-03-12 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}\n\n== Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's second visit to San Antonio and first since [[PFL 7 (2023 season)|PFL 7 (2023)]] in August 2023.\n\nThe event was headlined by a heavyweight bout between former interim [[Bellator Heavyweight Champion]] [[Valentin Moldavsky]] and [[2022 in Professional Fighters League#2022 PFL Heavyweight playoffs|2022 PFL heavyweight]] winner [[Ante Delija]].\n\nA trilogy clash between [[Bellator Women's Flyweight World Champion]] [[Liz Carmouche]] and former champion [[Juliana Velasquez]] took place at this event.\n\nFormer [[List of UFC champions#Women's Flyweight Championship|UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] title challenger [[Taila Santos]] was expected to face former [[Bellator Kickboxing#Women's Flyweight Championship|Bellator Kickboxing Women's Flyweight Champion]] [[Denise Kielholtz]] in a women's flyweight bout. However, a week before the event it was announced that Kielholtz had pulled out and replaced by Ilara Joanne.{{Cite web |date=2024-03-26 |title=Cappelozza removed from PFL card on April 4 |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/39816006/bruno-cappelozza-removed-pfl-fight-card-april-4 |access-date=2024-03-26 |website=ESPN.com |language=en}}\n\nOn March 26, changes to the fight lineup saw [[2021 in Professional Fighters League#2021 PFL Heavyweight playoffs|2021 PFL heavyweight]] winner [[Bruno Cappelozza]] replaced by former [[List of WSOF champions#Heavyweight Championship|WSOF Heavyweight Champion]] [[Blagoy Ivanov]] against Sergei Bilostenniy, and a PFL women's flyweight alternate bout between Kaytlin Neil and [[Sumiko Inaba]] removed from the card.\n\nOriginally, [[Daniel James (fighter)|Daniel James]] was expected to face Tyrell Fortune in a rematch at the event.{{cite web|url=https://combatpress.com/2024/03/pfl-announces-first-three-events-for-the-2024-regular-season/|title=PFL Announces First Three Events for the 2024 Regular Season|date=March 12, 2024|author=Timothy Wheaton|publisher=combatpress.com}} However, on the fight week it was announced that Fortune withdrew from the event and was replaced by [[Marcelo Golm]].{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Marcelo-Golm-Replaces-Tyrell-Fortune-Meets-Daniel-James-at-PFL-1-193167|title=Marcelo Golm Replaces Tyrell Fortune, Meets Daniel James at PFL 1|date=April 1, 2024|work=[[Sherdog]]|author=Tristen Critchfield}} At weigh-ins, [[Daniel James (fighter)|Daniel James]] came in at 267 lbs, 1 pound over the limit for heavyweight, while [[Shanna Young]] weighed in at 128.6 lbs, 2.6 pounds over the flyweight limit. They were fined a percentage of their purses and given a point deduction in the standings.{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=PFL 1 Weigh-in Results: Ante Delija, Valentin Moldavsky Official for Main Event |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/PFL-1-Weighin-Results-Ante-Delija-Valentin-Moldavsky-Official-for-Main-Event-193191 |access-date=2023-04-08 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN2 / ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Valentin Moldavsky]]\n|def.\n|[[Ante Delija]]\n|TKO (knees and end|notes=yes}}\n{{cite web |title=PFL 1 |url=https://pflmma.com/event/2024-pfl-1|website=www.pflmma.com |access-date=11 March 2024}}\n\n== Standings after event==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! 2024, at [[The Theater at Virgin Hotels]] in [[Las Vegas, Nevada]], United States. This marked the second regular-season event of the tournament and included fights in the [[Lightweight (MMA)|Lightweight]] and [[Light heavyweight (MMA)|Light Heavyweight]] divisions.{{Cite web |last=Cruz |first=Guilherme |date=2024-03-11 |title=PFL announces full line-ups for first 3 season events, 8 ex-Bellator champions debut |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/3/11/24097668/pfl-announces-full-line-up-three-season-events-debut-bellator-champions |access-date=2024-03-12 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}{{Cite web |date=2024-03-11 |title=PFL announces first three 2024 regular season lineups with influx of Bellator fighters, new women's division |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/2024-pfl-first-half-season-lineups-announced-bellator-influx-womens-flyweight-debut |access-date=2024-03-12 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=PFL Announces Full Cards for First Three Regular Season Events |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/PFL-Announces-Full-Cards-for-First-Three-Regular-Season-Events-192933 |access-date=2024-03-12 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}\n\n== Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's sixteenth visit to Las Vegas and first since [[PFL 3 (2023 season)|PFL 3 (2023)]] in April 2023.\n\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[Bellator Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Phil Davis (fighter)|Phil Davis]] and [[2022 in Professional Fighters League#2022 PFL Light Heavyweight playoffs|2022 PFL light heavyweight]] winner [[Rob Wilkinson]] was expected to headline the event. However, Davis withdraw from the bout for unknown reason and was replaced by [[Tom Breese]] at the co-main event. As a result, a light heavyweight bout between [[2023 in Professional Fighters League#2023 PFL Light Heavyweight playoffs|2023 PFL light heavyweight]] winner [[Impa Kasanganay]] and former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Light Heavyweight Championship|LFA Light Heavyweight Champion]] Alex Polizzi was promoted to main event status.{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=Tom Breese Replaces Phil Davis, Meets Rob Wilkinson at PFL 2 on April 12 |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Tom-Breese-Replaces-Phil-Davis-Meets-Rob-Wilkinson-at-PFL-2-on-April-12-193233 |access-date=2024-04-06 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}\n\nThe undercard also included the regular season, with the following fights being added as well: \n*A lightweight bout between former [[Bellator Lightweight Champion]] [[Patricky Pitbull]] and [[Clay Collard]].\n*A lightweight bout between [[Mads Burnell]] and Michael Dufort.\n*[[2022 in Professional Fighters League#2022 PFL Welterweight playoffs|2022 PFL welterweight]] winner [[Sadibou Sy]] made his a light heavyweight debut against Josh Silveira.\n*[[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 3]] heavyweight winner and [[2021 in Professional Fighters League#2021 PFL Light Heavyweight playoffs|2021 PFL light heavyweight]] winner [[Ant\u00f4nio Carlos J\u00fanior]] made his return from ACL injury against Simon Biyong in a light heavyweight bout.\n*A light heavyweight bout between [[Dovletdzhan Yagshimuradov]] and [[2023 in Professional Fighters League#Light Heavyweight|2023 PFL Europe light heavyweight]] winner Jakob Nedoh.\n*A lightweight bout between former [[Bellator Lightweight Champion]] [[Brent Primus]] and Bruno Miranda.\n*A lightweight bout between [[Gadzhi Rabadanov]] and Solomon Renfro.\n*Jay Jay Wilson was expected to face [[Adam Piccolotti]] in a lightweight bout, but Wilson pulled out and was replaced by Elvin Espinoza, who scheduled to face Anthony Romero in an alternate bout at this event.\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent |url=https://pflmma.com/event/2024-pfl-2|website=www.pflmma.com |access-date=11 March 2024}}\n\n== Standings after event==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Light heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! 2018.\n\nThe event was headlined by a welterweight bout between former [[Bellator Welterweight Champion]] [[Andrey Koreshkov]] and [[Magomed Umalatov]].\n\nThe undercard also included the regular season, with the following fights took place at the event:\n*A featherweight bout between [[2022 in Professional Fighters League#2022 PFL Featherweight playoffs|2022 PFL featherweight]] winner [[Brendan Loughnane]] and [[Pedro Carvalho (fighter)|Pedro Carvalho]].\n*A welterweight bout between former interim [[Bellator Welterweight Champion]] [[Logan Storley]] and Shamil Musaev.\n*A featherweight bout between Gabriel Alves Braga and former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Featherweight Championship|LFA Featherweight Champion]] Justin Gonzalez.\n*A welterweight rematch between [[Neiman Gracie]] and [[Goiti Yamauchi]].\n*A featherweight bout between former [[Brave Combat Federation#BRAVE Combat Federation Featherweight Championship|Brave CF Featherweight Champion]] [[Bubba Jenkins]] and [[Kai Kamaka III]].\n* [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America 2]] lightweight winner [[Enrique Barzola]] was return to featherweight division against [[\u00c1d\u00e1m Borics]].\n*A featherweight bout between [[Timur Khizriev]] and [[Brett Johns]].\n*Murad Ramazanov made his promotional debut against [[Laureano Staropoli]] in a welterweight bout. At weigh-ins, Staropoli came in at 175.2 lbs, 4.2 pounds over the limit for welterweight. He was fined 20% of his purse and given a point deduction in the standings.{{Cite web |date=2024-04-19 |title=2024 PFL 3 weigh-in results: Welterweights, featherweights set in Chicago (punches)|1|2:07|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n{{cite web |title=PFL 3|url=https://pflmma.com/event/2024-pfl-3|website=www.pflmma.com |access-date=11 March 2024}}\n\n== Standings after event==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Welterweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! eventually cancelled when Mowry withdrew. \n*In a women's flyweight bout, former [[LUX Fight League#Women's Strawweight Championship|LUX Women's Strawweight Champion]] Saray Orozco replaced Kaytlin Neil against Sumiko Inaba.\n*On May 20, it was announced that [[Davion Franklin]] was replacing Marcelo Nunes against Oleg Popov and [[Marcelo Golm]] was replacing [[Daniel James (fighter)|Daniel James]] against Tyrell Fortune.{{Cite web |date=2024-05-20 |title=Continuation of PFL's heavyweight season gets two changes in Connecticut 29\u201328)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n{{cite web |title=PFL 4|url=https://pflmma.com/event/2024-pfl-4|website=www.pflmma.com |access-date=April 22, 2024}}\n\n== Standings after event==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! end|notes=yes}}\n{{cite web |title=PFL 5|url=https://pflmma.com/event/2024-pfl-5|website=www.pflmma.com |access-date=April 29, 2024}}\n\n== Standings after event==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Light heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! 29\u201328)\n|3\n|5:00 \n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n{{cite web |title=PFL 6|url=https://pflmma.com/event/2024-pfl-6|website=www.pflmma.com |access-date=May 3, 2024}}\n\n== Standings after event==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Welterweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! League]] was held on August 2, 2024, at the [[Nashville Municipal Auditorium]] in [[Nashville, Tennessee]], United States. This marked the semi-final playoffs for the [[Flyweight (MMA)|Women's Flyweight]] and [[Heavyweight (MMA)|Heavyweight]] divisions.{{Cite web |date=2024-05-13 |title=The Professional Fighters League announces 2024 PFL Global season playoffs beginning August 2 |url=https://pflmma.com/news/the-professional-fighters-league-announces-2024-pfl-global-season-playoffs-beginning-august-2 |access-date=2024-05-23 |website=Professional Fighters League |language=en-US}}{{Cite web |last=Martin |first=Damon |date=2024-05-13 |title=PFL announces dates, locations for 2024 playoffs |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/5/13/24155490/pfl-announces-dates-locations-for-2024-playoffs |access-date=2024-05-23 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}{{Cite web |last=Anderson |first=Jay |date=2024-05-13 |title=PFL Announces Nashville, Hollywood, and Washington Playoff Dates |url=https://cagesidepress.com/2024/05/13/pfl-announces-nashville-hollywood-and-washington-playoff-dates/ |access-date=2024-05-23 |website=Cageside Press |language=en-US}}\n\n== Background ==\nThis event marked the promotion's first visit to Nashville and second in [[Tennessee]] state before it was rebranded promotion, since [[World Series of Fighting 27: Firmino vs. Fodor|WSOF 27]] in [[Memphis, Tennessee|Memphis]] in January 2016.{{Cite web |date=2024-05-13 |title=PFL announces dates, locations for 2024 playoff events |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/05/pfl-2024-playoff-dates-locations-nashville-hollywood-florida-washington |access-date=2024-05-23 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nThe event featured the semifinal of 2024 PFL playoffs in a heavyweight and women's flyweight divisions. [[Oleg Popov (fighter)|Oleg Popov]] was originally scheduled to face [[Valentin Moldavsky]], but Moldavsky was forced to withdraw due to injury and was replaced by [[Linton Vassell]].{{Cite web |date=2024-07-09 |title=2024 PFL Playoffs main cards announced including notable semifinal switch |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/2024-pfl-playoffs-main-card-announced-including-notable-semifinal-switch |access-date=2024-07-09 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nAt weigh-ins;{{Cite web |last=Staff |date=2024-08-01 |title=PFL Nashville: Goltsov vs. Johnson Weigh-In Results \u2014 Carmouche Heavy |url=https://cagesidepress.com/2024/08/01/pfl-nashville-goltsov-vs-johnson-weigh-in-results-carmouche-heavy/ |access-date=2024-08-02 |website=Cageside Press |language=en-US}}\n\n* [[Liz Carmouche]] missed weight by 1 pound, coming in at 127 pounds. Carmouche forfeited 20 percent of her purse and entered the bout with a 1-pound penalty on the scorecards.\n* Sergio Cossio missed weight by 6.8 pounds at 162.8 pounds, for his lightweight fight with Dedrek Sanders. The fight moved forward, and Cossio paid a penalty.\n* Willis (172.6 pounds) and Ivy (172.8 pounds) both missed weight. 2023.{{Cite web |date=2024-01-18 |title=PFL Europe 2024 Dates Announced in Newcastle and Glasgow Along With Massive Matchup Coming to Paris |url=https://www.combatsportsuk.co.uk/pfl-europe-2024-dates-announced-in-newcastle-and-glasgow/ |access-date=2024-04-18 |website=Combat Sports UK |language=en-GB}}\n\nA 160-pounds catchweight bout between current [[Undisputed championship (boxing)|undisputed female super-middleweight boxing]] world champion [[Savannah Marshall]] and Mirela Vargas headlined the event.{{Cite web |date=2024-04-22 |title=Savannah Marshall's MMA debut opponent revealed as full 2024 PFL Europe 2 lineup announced |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/04/2024-pfl-europe-2-savannah-marshalls-opponent-full-card |access-date=2024-04-23 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nThis event featured the quarterfinal of 2024 PFL Europe season in a bantamweight and women's flyweight division. This also the latest quarterfinal welterweight division between Jack Grant and Charlie Leary, the winner faced Ibrahim Man\u00e9 in the semifinal bout.{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=Full Card Announced for PFL Europe Newcastle on June 8 |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Full-Card-Announced-for-PFL-Europe-Newcastle-on-June-8-193426/amp |access-date=2024-04-23 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}\n\nAt weigh-ins, Dee Begley missed the flyweight limit by 4.3 pounds, while Ayton De Paepe proceeded the bantamweight limit by 1.5 pounds. Consequently, Begley was fined 30% of her purse to her opponent, Paulina Wisniewska, and begin the fight with a point deduction. On the other hand, De Paepe failed his medical exams, leading to the cancellation of his fight. As a result, his opponent, Dean Garnett, advanced to the PFL Europe playoffs and receive both his show and win checks.{{Cite web |last=Guillen |first=Adam |date=2024-06-07 |title=PFL Europe Newcastle weigh ins results, staredown videos {{!}} Marshall vs Vargas |url=https://www.mmamania.com/2024/6/7/24171640/pfl-newcastle-weigh-ins-results-staredown-videos-marshall-vs-vargas-dazn-europe-mma |access-date=2024-06-07 |website=MMAmania.com |language=en}}{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=PFL Europe 2 Weigh-in Results: Savannah Marshall Set for MMA Debut |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/PFL-Europe-2-Weighin-Results-Savannah-Marshall-Set-for-MMA-Debut-193943 |access-date=2024-06-07 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}\n\n== Results |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\n==Background ==\nIn late August, 2023, the promotion announced that it had sold a minority stake for $100 million to SRJ Sports Investments \u2013 a branch of Public Investment Fund \u2013 to invest into creating PFL MENA (Middle East and North Africa) regional league. Starting in 2024, PFL MENA will feature the top emerging Middle East and North African fighters, and will be broadcast during prime local hours with all events staged in the MENA region and live stream on [[DAZN]]. The format will follow the same as the regular PFL Season, with the winner receiving a $100,000 prize and a chance to earn a spot in the 2025 PFL regular season.{{Cite web |last=Guillen |first=Adam |date=2024-02-24 |title=PFL expands on global reach with launch of PFL MENA |url=https://www.mmamania.com/2024/2/24/24081864/pfl-expands-global-expansion-launch-pfl-mena-saudia-arabia-middle-east-espn-mma |access-date=2024-04-23 |website=MMAmania.com |language=en}}{{Cite web |last=Islam |first=Arif |date=2024-02-27 |title=PFL MENA league to launch in April |url=https://www.sportspro.com/news/pfl-mena-league-season-srj-middle-east-mma/ |access-date=2024-04-23 |website=SportsPro |language=en-GB}}{{Cite web |last=McAuley |first=John |date=2024-02-27 |title=What is PFL Mena and where will the MMA 'Champions League' be held? |url=https://www.thenationalnews.com/sport/combat-sports/2024/02/27/what-is-pfl-mena-and-where-will-the-mma-champions-league-be-held/ |access-date=2024-04-23 |website=The National |language=en}}\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (DAZN)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|Abdullah Al-Qahtani \n|def.\n|Yazeed Hasanain\n|TKO (submission to punches)\n|2\n|4:59\n|[[#Featherweight|2024 PFL MENA Featherweight Tournament]] Quarterfinal. Finale.jpg\n|image_size=\n|promotion=[[Professional Fighters League]]\n|date=November 24, 2023\n|venue=[[The Anthem (music venue)|The Anthem]]\n|city=[[Washington, D.C.]], [[United 10''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Professional Fighters League]] that took place on November 24, 2023, at [[The Anthem (music venue)|The Anthem]] in [[Washington, D.C.]], [[United States]]. This was the finale of the playoffs for all 2023 divisions, with the winners getting a PFL World Championship and a $1 million cash prize.{{Cite web |last=Anderson |first=Jay |date=2023-08-23 |title=2023 PFL Championship Announced for Washington D.C on November 24 |url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/08/23/2023-pfl-championships-announced-washington-d-c-november-24/ |access-date=2023-08-26 |website=Cageside Press |language=en-US}}{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=2023 PFL Championships to Be Held in Washington, D.C. on Nov. 24 |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/2023-PFL-Championships-to-Be-Held-in-Washington-DC-on-Nov-24-190843 |access-date=2023-08-26 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe event marked the promotion's fourth visit to Washington, D.C. and first since [[PFL 10 (2018 season)]] in October 2018.\n\nThe event featured the six matchups for the $1 million dollar PFL World Championships in the 2023 season included a featherweight title bout between [[Jesus Pinedo]] and Gabriel Alves Braga in a rematch, a lightweight title bout between 2022 champion [[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]] and [[Clay Collard]], a welterweight title bout between 2022 champion [[Sadibou Sy]] and 2018 champion [[Magomed Magomedkerimov]], a light heavyweight bout between Josh Silveira and [[Impa Kasanganay]], a heavyweight title bout between [[Denis Goltsov]] and [[Renan Ferreira (fighter)|Renan Ferreira]], and a women's featherweight title bout between 2022 women's lightweight champion [[Larissa Pacheco]] and [[Marina Mokhnatkina]].{{Cite web |last=Heck |first=Mike |date=2023-09-20 |title=PFL announces full 2023 World Championship pay-per-view card |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/9/20/23882036/pfl-announces-full-2023-world-championship-pay-per-view-card |access-date=2023-10-23 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}\n\nA women's featherweight bout between [[2019 in Professional Fighters League#2021 PFL Women's Lightweight playoffs|2019]] and [[2021 in Professional Fighters League#2021 PFL Women's Lightweight playoffs|2021 PFL Women's Lightweight Champion]] [[Kayla Harrison]] and former [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Women's Featherweight World Championship|Bellator Women's Featherweight Champion]] [[Julia Budd]] was expected to take place at the event.{{cite web |last1=Burne |first1=Kathrine |title=Kayla Harrison Returns Against Julia Budd At PFL 10 |url=https://jitsmagazine.com/kayla-harrison-returns-against-julia-budd-at-pfl-10/ |website=Jitsmagazine |access-date=13 September 2023}} However, Budd was removed from the fight because she \"refused to fulfill her contractual obligation\" and was replaced by [[Aspen Ladd]] at a catchweight of 150 pounds.{{Cite web |last=Martin |first=Damon |date=2023-11-08 |title=Kayla Harrison now faces UFC veteran Aspen Ladd with Julia Budd out of PFL Finals card |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/11/8/23953015/kayla-harrison-now-faces-ufc-veteran-aspen-ladd-with-julia-budd-out-of-pfl-finals-card |access-date=2023-11-09 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Chris Wade (fighter)|Chris Wade]] and [[Bubba Jenkins]] was scheduled at this event; however at weigh-ins, Wade came in at 148.4 pounds, which was 2.4 pounds north of the non-title. Jenkins turned down the catchweight bout and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web |date=2023-11-23 |title=Bubba Jenkins turns down catchweight bout after Chris Wade's weight miss at 2023 PFL Championship |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/11/2023-pfl-championship-bubba-jenkins-vs-chris-wade-canceled-weight-miss-rivalry-trilogy-bad-blood |access-date=2023-11-24 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nAt weigh-ins, [[Ray Cooper III]] and Josh Blyden missed weight, coming in at 186.8 pounds, .8 pounds over the limit, and at 146.8 pounds, .8 pounds over the limit, respectively. Both were fined percentages of their purses which went to their opponents and the bouts proceeded at catchweights.{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=2023 Season PFL Championships Weigh-in Results: Wade vs. Jenkins 3 Scratched |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/2023-Season-PFL-Championships-Weighin-Results-Wade-vs-Jenkins-3-Scratched-191821 |access-date=2023-11-24 |website=Sherdog Background ==\n\nThis event was headlined by returning 2022 Lightweight Champion [[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]] facing debutant Anthony Romero and 2022 Welterweight Champion [[Sadibou Sy]] facing off against Shane Mitchell.\n\nA welterweight bout between [[C\u00e9dric Doumb\u00e9|Cedric Doumb\u00e9]] and Jarrah Al-Silawi was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web |last=Lee |first=Alexander K. |date=2023-05-12 |title=PFL announces second-half event lineups following 9 fighters receiving drug test suspensions |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/5/12/23721590/pfl-announces-second-half-event-lineups-following-9-fighters-receiving-drug-test-suspensions |access-date=2023-05-14 |website=MMA Fighting PFL debut due to wrist injury |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/6/7/23752525/cedric-doumbe-out-of-pfl-debut-due-to-wrist-injury |access-date=2023-06-08 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}} Juusola was later replaced by Solomon Renfro.\n\nAt weigh-ins, Dilano Taylor weighed in at 171.2 pounds, 0.2 pounds over the Welterweight limit. He was fined 20 percent of his purse which went to his opponent Carlos Leal and he was given a one-point penalty in the standings. Brahyan Zurcher also missed weight for his featherweight bout against Mike Bardsley, weighing in at 146.8 pounds and forfeited 20 percent of his purse to Bardsley.{{Cite web |date=2023-06-22 event ==\nThe PFL points system is based on the results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight to be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Welterweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! This marked the semi-final playoffs for the [[Featherweight]] and [[Light heavyweight|Light Heavyweight]] divisions.{{Cite web |last=Newswire |first=MMA Fighting |date=2023-06-29 |title=PFL announces full playoff card for Aug. 4 with featherweights and light heavyweights competing |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/6/29/23778471/pfl-announces-full-playoff-card-for-aug-4-with-featherweights-and-light-heavyweights-competing |access-date=2023-06-30 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}{{Cite web |date=2023-06-30 |title=PFL announces featherweight, light heavyweight playoff matchups for San Antonio |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/06/pfl-playoffs-2023-featherweight-light-heavyweight-matchups-san-antonio |access-date=2023-06-30 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=PFL Sets 10-Bout Lineup for First 2023 Playoffs Event on Aug. 4 |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/PFL-Sets-10Bout-Lineup-for-First-2023-Playoffs-Event-on-Aug-4-190384 |access-date=2023-06-30 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe event was headlined by [[Bubba Jenkins]] facing off against [[Jesus Pinedo]] in an attempt to reach the featherweight finals. Pinedo secured his playoff spot after stunningly knocking out [[Brendan Loughnane]], the 2022 PFL champion, in the first round. Jenkins, who lost his bid for the PFL title to Loughnane in 2022, aimed to make it to the finals again this August.\n\nThe featherweight bracket's other pairing was to involve former PFL champion [[Movlid Khaybulaev]] and Gabriel Braga, battling for a spot in this year's finals. However Khaybulaev pulled out due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[Chris Wade (fighter)|Chris Wade]].{{Cite web |last=Al-Shatti |first=Shaun |date=2023-07-19 |title=Chris Wade replaces Movlid Khaybulaev in PFL welterweight playoffs|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/7/19/23800651/chris-wade-replaces-movlid-khaybulaev-in-pfl-welterweight-playoffs |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}\n\nIn the light heavyweight division, Josh Silveira competed against Ty Flores, and Marthin Hamlet squared off with ex-UFC fighter [[Impa Kasanganay]].\n\nAt weigh-ins, [[Jesus Pinedo]] weighed in at 146.4 pounds, 0.4 pounds over the Featherweight limit. He was fined 20 percent of his purse, which went to his opponent Jenkins, and he started the bout with a one point subtraction. This featured the semi-final playoffs for the [[Heavyweight]] and Women's [[Featherweight]] divisions.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/37998206/pfl-sets-final-two-playoff-dates-matchups-held-ny|title=PFL sets final two playoff dates, matchups to be held in N.Y.|date=July 12, 2023|work=[[ESPN]]|author=Marc Raimondi}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe event was headlined by [[Renan Ferreira (fighter)|Renan Ferreira]] facing off against [[Maurice Greene (fighter)|Maurice Greene]] in an attempt to reach the heavyweight finals. Ferreira was initially scheduled to face number three seed Marcelo Nunes, but he was forced to withdraw due to an injury and was replaced by fifth-seed Greene.{{cite web|url=https://www.pflmma.com/news/2023-pfl-playoff-cards-finalized-for-new-york-city-august-18-23|title=2023 PFL PLAYOFF CARDS FINALIZED FOR NEW YORK CITY AUGUST 18 & 23|date=July 12, 2023|work=[[Professional Fighters League]]}}\n\nAt weigh-ins, Olena Kolesnyk weighed in at 147.8 pounds, 1.8 pounds over the Featherweight limit. She was fined 20 percent of his purse, which went to her opponent Pacheco, and she started the bout with a one-point subtraction. Daiqwon Buckley weighed in at 272.4 pounds, 6.4 pounds over the Heavyweight limit, leading him to being fined 20% of his purse which went to his opponent Sutherland. The bout was briefly moved to Super Heavyweight before the promotion cancelled it. The [[New York State Athletic Commission]] decided not to license Chris Mixan, leading to his lightweight bout against Eddie George to be scrapped.{{Cite web |date=2023-08-17 |title=2023 PFL Playoffs 2 weigh-in results: Postseason intact, but miss moves showcase bout to super heavyweight |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/08/2023-pfl-playoffs-2-weigh-in-results-postseason-intact-but-miss-moves-showcase-bout-to-super-heavyweight |access-date=2023-08-17 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN / ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Renan Ferreira (fighter)|Renan Ferreira]]\n|def.\n|[[Maurice Greene (fighter)|Maurice Greene]]\n|KO (punches)\n|1\n|4:46\n|2023 PFL Heavyweight Tournament Semifinal.\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Featherweight\n|[[Larissa Pacheco]]\n|def.\n|Olena Kolesnyk\n|TKO (punches)\n|1\n|0:14\n|2023 PFL Women's Featherweight Tournament Semifinal.
